Login.jsx :

// src/pages/Login.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { BASE_URL } from '../services/api';

const Login = () => {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const handleLogin = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setIsLoading(true);

    try {
      // Appel direct à l'API Auth
      const response = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/api/Auth/login`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error("Email ou mot de passe incorrect.");
      }

      const data = await response.json();

      // BINGO ! On stocke le Token et l'ID dans le navigateur
      localStorage.setItem('jwtToken', data.token);
      localStorage.setItem('userId', data.userId);
      localStorage.setItem('companyName', data.companyName);

      // Téléportation vers le Dashboard
      navigate('/');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-bg flex items-center justify-center p-4 font-sans">
      <div className="bg-white p-10 rounded-[2rem] shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md border border-slate-100 animate-in zoom-in duration-300">
        
        <div className="text-center mb-10">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-dark mb-2">Digi<span className="text-brand-red">Alert</span></h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Connectez-vous à votre espace pro</p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="bg-rose-50 text-brand-red p-4 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold mb-6 text-center border border-rose-200">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-6">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Adresse E-mail</label>
            <input
              type="email"
              required
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 focus:border-brand-red transition-all"
              placeholder="drkoffi@clinique.com"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Mot de passe</label>
            <input
              type="password"
              required
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 focus:border-brand-red transition-all"
              placeholder="••••••••"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={isLoading}
            className="w-full bg-brand-dark hover:bg-slate-800 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-lg active:scale-95 flex justify-center mt-4"
          >
            {isLoading ? 'Connexion en cours...' : 'Se connecter'}
          </button>
        </form>
        
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Login;
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NouveauRdv.jsx :

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { MessageSquare, Calendar as CalendarIcon, Check, X, Plus } from 'lucide-react';

const NouveauRdv = () => {
  // États pour gérer l'ouverture des 3 Pop-ups
  const [showMsgModal, setShowMsgModal] = useState(false);
  const [showRecurrenceModal, setShowRecurrenceModal] = useState(false);
  const [showValidationModal, setShowValidationModal] = useState(false);

  // État des données du formulaire (pour le résumé de validation)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    titre: '',
    clients: ['Dr. Koffi'],
    date: '',
    heure: '',
    messageSMS: '',
    messageEmail: '',
    recurrences: [],
    emailEnabled: true
  });

  // Récurrence rows temporaires pour le modal
  const [recurrenceRows, setRecurrenceRows] = useState([
    { id: 1, date: '', heure: '', repeat: 'Non' }
  ]);

  // Message modal selections
  const [selectedMsgId, setSelectedMsgId] = useState(1);
  const [msgFields, setMsgFields] = useState({ nom: '', prenom: '', date: '' });
  // Clients dropdown
  const AVAILABLE_CLIENTS = ['Dr. Koffi', 'M. Amida', 'Mme. Aminata'];
  const [showClientDropdown, setShowClientDropdown] = useState(false);
  const handleConfirm = async () => {
  const startDateTime = `${formData.date}T${formData.heure}:00`;
  const evtRes = await fetchWithAuth('/api/Events', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify({
      title: formData.titre,
      startDateTime,
      endDateTime: startDateTime, // ajuste selon ta logique de durée
      contactIds: formData.clients.map(/* mapper vers de vrais IDs contact, pas des noms */),
    }),
  });
  if (!evtRes.ok) return;
  const { eventId } = await evtRes.json();

  // Un rappel par contact (adapte si récurrences multiples)
  await fetchWithAuth('/api/Reminders', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify({
      idEvent: eventId,
      idContact: /* id du contact */,
      scheduledTime: startDateTime,
      messageText: formData.messageSMS,
    }),
  });

  setShowValidationModal(false);
  // reset du formulaire...
};
  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto p-10 bg-brand-bg relative">
      
      {/* Titre de la page */}
      <h1 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-dark mb-8 shrink-0">
        Agenda <span className="text-slate-400 font-medium">&gt; Nouveau RDV</span>
      </h1>

      {/* Conteneur Principal (Les 2 Colonnes) */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-[0_4px_12px_rgba(15,23,42,0.05)] border border-slate-100 p-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-12">
        
        {/* ================= COLONNE GAUCHE (Infos RDV) ================= */}
        <div className="flex-1 space-y-6">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Titre</label>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Titre du rapelle" className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-full focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 outline-none" onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, titre: e.target.value})} />
          </div>

          <div className="relative">
            <label className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Client(s)</label>
            <div>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowClientDropdown(!showClientDropdown)} className="w-full text-left px-4 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-full flex justify-between items-center">
                <span className="text-slate-700">
                  {formData.clients && formData.clients.length > 0 ? `${formData.clients.length} client(s) sélectionné(s)` : 'Sélectionner un client'}
                </span>
                <span className="text-slate-400">▾</span>
              </button>

              {showClientDropdown && (
                <div className="absolute z-40 mt-2 w-full bg-white border rounded-lg shadow-md max-h-48 overflow-auto">
                  {AVAILABLE_CLIENTS.map((c, i) => (
                    <div key={i} className="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer flex justify-between items-center" onClick={() => {
                      const exists = formData.clients && formData.clients.includes(c);
                      if (!exists) setFormData({...formData, clients: [...(formData.clients||[]), c]});
                      setShowClientDropdown(false);
                    }}>
                      <span>{c}</span>
                      {formData.clients && formData.clients.includes(c) ? <span className="text-sm text-slate-400">✓</span> : null}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {formData.clients && formData.clients.map((c, i) => (
                <span key={i} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 bg-slate-100 rounded-full text-sm">
                  <span className="truncate max-w-[12rem]">{c}</span>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => { const copy = formData.clients.filter(x => x !== c); setFormData({...formData, clients: copy}); }} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700"><X className="w-3 h-3" /></button>
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-6">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Date</label>
              <input type="date" value={formData.date} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, date: e.target.value, recurrences: []})} disabled={formData.recurrences && formData.recurrences.length > 0} className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-full outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 disabled:opacity-60" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Heure</label>
              <input type="time" value={formData.heure} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, heure: e.target.value, recurrences: []})} disabled={formData.recurrences && formData.recurrences.length > 0} className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-full outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 disabled:opacity-60" />
            </div>
          </div>
          {formData.recurrences && formData.recurrences.length > 0 && (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-brand-red">La date principale est désactivée car une récurrence est programmée.</p>
          )}
          <div className="pt-4 flex flex-col items-center border-t border-slate-100">
              <button onClick={() => setShowRecurrenceModal(true)} className="px-6 py-2 bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold rounded-full shadow-md hover:bg-slate-800 transition-all mb-4">
                Récurrence ?
              </button>
            <div className="w-full p-6 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-2xl">
              {formData.recurrences && formData.recurrences.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {formData.recurrences.map((r, idx) => (
                    <div key={idx} className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border">
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold">{idx+1}. {r.date || '—'} à {r.heure || '—'}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Répète : {r.repeat}</div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-28">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 font-bold">Pas de Recurrence Defini</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Ligne Séparatrice Verticale */}
        <div className="hidden md:block w-px bg-brand-red opacity-30"></div>

        {/* ================= COLONNE DROITE (Automatisations) ================= */}
        <div className="flex-1 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Bloc SMS */}
          <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 relative">
            <h3 className="flex items-center text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark absolute -top-3.5 left-4 bg-white px-2">
              <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" /> Rapelle SMS
            </h3>
            <textarea 
              placeholder="Message" 
              className="w-full h-24 mt-2 resize-none text-2xl text-slate-400 outline-none"
              onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, messageSMS: e.target.value})}
              value={formData.messageSMS}
            ></textarea>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-center">
            <button onClick={() => setShowMsgModal(true)} className="px-6 py-2 bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold rounded-full shadow-md hover:bg-slate-800 transition-all">
              Message Programmé ?
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Bloc Email */}
          <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 relative mt-10">
            <div className="absolute -top-4 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 bg-brand-dark text-white px-6 py-1.5 rounded-full flex items-center space-x-3 shadow-md">
              <span className="font-bold">Email ?</span>
              <input type="checkbox" checked={formData.emailEnabled} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, emailEnabled: e.target.checked})} className="w-4 h-4 accent-brand-red cursor-pointer" />
            </div>
            <input type="email" placeholder="Email" disabled={!formData.emailEnabled} value={formData.email || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, email: e.target.value})} className="w-full mt-4 px-4 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-full outline-none focus:border-brand-dark disabled:opacity-40" />
            
              <div className={`border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 mt-4 relative ${!formData.emailEnabled ? 'opacity-60 pointer-events-none' : ''}`}>
                <h3 className="flex items-center text-md font-bold text-brand-dark absolute -top-3 left-4 bg-white px-2">
                  <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" /> Rapelle Email
                </h3>
                <textarea placeholder="Message" disabled={!formData.emailEnabled} className="w-full h-20 mt-2 resize-none text-2xl text-slate-400 outline-none disabled:opacity-40" value={formData.messageEmail} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, messageEmail: e.target.value})}></textarea>
              </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-center pt-4">
            <button onClick={() => setShowValidationModal(true)} className="px-10 py-3 bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-slate-800 transition-all active:scale-95">
              Programmer
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ================= 1. POP-UP MESSAGE PROGRAMMÉ ================= */}
      {showMsgModal && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-dark/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl p-8 w-full max-w-2xl border-t-4 border-brand-red animate-in zoom-in duration-200">
            <div className="flex gap-8">
              <div className="flex-1 border-r border-slate-100 pr-6">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-center mb-4">Liste Messages</h3>
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <button onClick={() => setSelectedMsgId(1)} className={`w-full py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-full font-bold text-brand-dark hover:bg-slate-100 flex items-center px-4 ${selectedMsgId===1 ? 'bg-slate-50 shadow-md':''}`}>
                    <span className="mr-4">1</span> Mess Rendez-vous
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => setSelectedMsgId(2)} className={`w-full py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-slate-100 flex items-center px-4 ${selectedMsgId===2 ? 'bg-slate-50 shadow-md':''}`}>
                    <span className="mr-4">2</span> Mess Rappelle RDV
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-center mb-4">Champs</h3>
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  <div className="flex space-x-2">
                    <div className="flex-1"><label className="text-xs font-bold pl-2">Nom</label><input type="text" placeholder="Nom" className="w-full border rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm" value={msgFields.nom} onChange={e=>setMsgFields({...msgFields, nom: e.target.value})} disabled={formData.clients && formData.clients.length>1} /></div>
                    <div className="flex-1"><label className="text-xs font-bold pl-2">Prenom</label><input type="text" placeholder="Prénom" className="w-full border rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm" value={msgFields.prenom} onChange={e=>setMsgFields({...msgFields, prenom: e.target.value})} disabled={formData.clients && formData.clients.length>1} /></div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="text-xs font-bold pl-2">Date</label>
                    <div className="relative w-1/2">
                      <CalendarIcon className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-brand-red w-4 h-4"/>
                      <input type="date" value={msgFields.date} onChange={e=>setMsgFields({...msgFields, date: e.target.value})} className="w-full pl-10 pr-3 py-1 border rounded-full text-sm"/>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center">
              <button onClick={() => {
                const templates = {
                  1: "Bonjour {prenom} {nom}, ceci est un rappel de votre rendez-vous le {date}.",
                  2: "Rappel: votre rendez-vous est programmé le {date}."
                };
                let msg = templates[selectedMsgId] || templates[1];
                if (formData.clients && formData.clients.length>1) {
                  msg = msg.replace(/\{prenom\}|\{nom\}|\{date\}/g, '').replace(/ +/g, ' ').trim();
                } else {
                  msg = msg.replace('{prenom}', msgFields.prenom || '').replace('{nom}', msgFields.nom || '').replace('{date}', msgFields.date || formData.date || '');
                }
                setFormData({...formData, messageSMS: msg});
                setShowMsgModal(false);
              }} className="px-8 py-2 bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold rounded-full flex items-center"><Check className="w-5 h-5 mr-2 text-green-400"/> Valider</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ================= 2. POP-UP RÉCURRENCE ================= */}
      {showRecurrenceModal && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-dark/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl p-8 w-full max-w-xl border-b-4 border-brand-red animate-in zoom-in duration-200">
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center mb-6">Programmer une Récurrence</h3>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {recurrenceRows.map((row, idx) => (
                <div key={row.id} className={`flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center gap-3 border ${idx===1? 'border-brand-red':'border-slate-200'} p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm`}>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className="font-bold ml-2">{idx+1}</span>
                    <span className="font-bold">Date</span>
                    <input type="date" value={row.date} onChange={e => { const copy=[...recurrenceRows]; copy[idx].date = e.target.value; setRecurrenceRows(copy); }} className="border rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-sm outline-none min-w-0"/>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className="font-bold">Heure</span>
                    <input type="time" value={row.heure} onChange={e => { const copy=[...recurrenceRows]; copy[idx].heure = e.target.value; setRecurrenceRows(copy); }} className="border rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-sm outline-none min-w-0"/>
                    <div className="text-xs font-bold">Répéter
                      <select value={row.repeat} onChange={e => { const copy=[...recurrenceRows]; copy[idx].repeat = e.target.value; setRecurrenceRows(copy); }} className="border rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-sm ml-2">
                        <option>Non</option>
                        <option>Jour</option>
                        <option>Semaine</option>
                        <option>Mois</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-center mt-4 text-brand-red">
              <Plus onClick={() => { const nextId = recurrenceRows.length ? recurrenceRows[recurrenceRows.length-1].id + 1 : 1; setRecurrenceRows([...recurrenceRows, { id: nextId, date: '', heure: '', repeat: 'Non' }]); }} className="w-6 h-6 cursor-pointer hover:scale-110 transition-transform" />
            </div>
            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
              <button onClick={() => { setFormData({...formData, recurrences: recurrenceRows, date: '', heure: ''}); setShowRecurrenceModal(false); }} className="px-8 py-2 bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold rounded-full flex items-center"><Check className="w-5 h-5 mr-2 text-green-400"/> Valider</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ================= 3. POP-UP VALIDATION FINALE ================= */}
      {showValidationModal && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-dark/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md p-8 animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 text-center border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">Résumé de la Programmation</h2>
            
            <div className="space-y-3 mb-8 text-slate-700">
              <p><span className="font-bold text-brand-dark">Titre :</span> {formData.titre || '-'}</p>

              <div>
                <span className="font-bold text-brand-dark">Clients :</span>
                <ul className="mt-1 list-disc list-inside text-slate-700">
                  {formData.clients && formData.clients.length>0 ? formData.clients.map((c, i) => (
                    <li key={i}>{c} — id: client-{i+1}</li>
                  )) : <li>-</li>}
                </ul>
              </div>

              <p><span className="font-bold text-brand-dark">Date :</span> {formData.date ? `${formData.date} à ${formData.heure || '-'}` : '-'}</p>

              {formData.recurrences && formData.recurrences.length>0 && (
                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">Récurrences :</p>
                  <ul className="list-disc list-inside text-slate-700">
                    {formData.recurrences.map((r,i) => (
                      <li key={i}>{r.date || '-'} à {r.heure || '-'} — {r.repeat}</li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 mt-2">
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-dark mb-1">Aperçu SMS :</p>
                <p className="text-sm italic text-slate-600">"{formData.messageSMS || 'Aucun message configuré.'}"</p>
              </div>

              {formData.emailEnabled && (formData.email || formData.messageEmail) && (
                <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 mt-2">
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-dark mb-1">Email :</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-700">{formData.email || '-'}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-dark mb-1 mt-2">Aperçu Email :</p>
                  <p className="text-sm italic text-slate-600">"{formData.messageEmail || 'Aucun message configuré.'}"</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-center space-x-4">
              <button onClick={() => setShowValidationModal(false)} className="px-6 py-2 border-2 border-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-bold rounded-full hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors">
                Modifier
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => {
                setShowValidationModal(false);
                alert("RDV et Rappels enregistrés en BDD !");
                // Reset form
                setFormData({ titre: '', clients: [], date: '', heure: '', messageSMS: '', messageEmail: '', recurrences: [], emailEnabled: true });
                setRecurrenceRows([{ id: 1, date: '', heure: '', repeat: 'Non' }]);
                setMsgFields({ nom: '', prenom: '', date: '' });
                setSelectedMsgId(1);
              }} className="px-6 py-2 bg-brand-red text-white font-bold rounded-full hover:bg-rose-700 shadow-md transition-colors">
                Confirmer l'envoi
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default NouveauRdv;

Parametres.jsx :

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { User, Plug, FileText, Eye, EyeOff, Edit2, Trash2, X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

const Parametres = () => {
  // Gestion de l'onglet actif (profil, api, templates)
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('api');
  // Gestion de l'affichage du mot de passe / Clé API
  const [showApiKey, setShowApiKey] = useState(false);
  // Gestion du Pop-up "Nouveau Modèle"
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  // Gestion des templates
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState([
    { id: 1, name: 'Mess Rendez-vous', content: 'Bonjour {Prenom} {Nom}, ceci est un rappel de votre rendez-vous le {Date}.' },
    { id: 2, name: 'Mess Rendez-vous1', content: 'Bonjour {Prenom}, votre rendez-vous est prévu le {Date}.' },
    { id: 3, name: 'Mess Rendez-vous2', content: 'Rappel: rendez-vous le {Date} à {Heure}.' },
    { id: 4, name: 'Mess Rendez-vous3', content: 'Bonjour {Nom}, vous avez un rendez-vous le {Date}.' },
    { id: 5, name: 'Mess Rendez-vous4', content: 'Votre prochain rendez-vous est le {Date}.' },
    { id: 6, name: 'Mess Rendez-vous5', content: 'Nouveau rappel: rendez-vous le {Date}.' },
  ]);
  const [editingTemplate, setEditingTemplate] = useState(null);
  const [modelName, setModelName] = useState('');
  const [messageContent, setMessageContent] = useState('');
  const [connectionStatus, setConnectionStatus] = useState('idle');
  const [connectionMessage, setConnectionMessage] = useState('Cliquez pour tester votre connexion.');
  const [loadingConnection, setLoadingConnection] = useState(false);
  const [confirmDeleteId, setConfirmDeleteId] = useState(null);
  const [dragOverField, setDragOverField] = useState(false);
  const fields = ['Nom', 'Prenom', 'Perso1', 'Perso2'];

  const testConnection = () => {
    if (loadingConnection) return;
    setLoadingConnection(true);
    setConnectionStatus('pending');
    setConnectionMessage('Test de connexion en cours...');
    setTimeout(() => {
      const success = Math.random() > 0.35;
      setConnectionStatus(success ? 'success' : 'error');
      setConnectionMessage(success ? 'Connexion réussie au serveur DigiSMS' : 'Échec de la connexion, vérifiez la clé API.');
      setLoadingConnection(false);
    }, 1400);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto p-10 bg-brand-bg relative">

      <div className="flex gap-10">
        
        {/* ================= MENU GAUCHE (ONGLETS) ================= */}
        <div className="w-1/3 flex flex-col space-y-4">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('profil')}
            className={`flex items-center px-6 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg transition-all ${activeTab === 'profil' ? 'bg-slate-400 text-white shadow-md' : 'bg-slate-300/60 text-slate-800'}`}
          >
            <User className="w-6 h-6 mr-3" /> Profil de l'Entreprise
          </button>
          
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('api')}
            className={`flex items-center px-6 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg transition-all ${activeTab === 'api' ? 'bg-slate-400 text-white shadow-md' : 'bg-slate-300/60 text-slate-800'}`}
          >
            <Plug className="w-6 h-6 mr-3" /> Intégration API (DigiSMS)
          </button>

          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('templates')}
            className={`flex items-center px-6 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg transition-all ${activeTab === 'templates' ? 'bg-slate-400 text-white shadow-md' : 'bg-slate-300/60 text-slate-800'}`}
          >
            <FileText className="w-6 h-6 mr-3" /> Modèles de SMS (Templates)
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* ================= CONTENU DROITE (CARTE BLANCHE) ================= */}
        <div className="w-2/3 bg-white rounded-[2rem] shadow-lg border border-slate-100 p-10 min-h-[500px]">
          
          {/* ---- ONGLET : PROFIL ---- */}
          {activeTab === 'profil' && (
            <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-300">
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center mb-8">
                <h2 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-brand-dark flex items-center">
                  <User className="w-8 h-8 mr-3" /> Profil de l'Entreprise
                </h2>
                <div className="w-48 h-1 bg-brand-red rounded-full mt-3"></div>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Nom de la Structure</label>
                  <input type="text" defaultValue="Clinique Biova" className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-400 rounded-full text-brand-dark focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 outline-none" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Adresse E-mail de connexion</label>
                  <input type="email" defaultValue="drkoffi@cliniquebiova.com" className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-400 rounded-full text-brand-dark focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 outline-none" />
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-center pt-8">
                  <button className="px-8 py-3 bg-white text-brand-dark font-extrabold text-xl rounded-2xl border-b-4 border-brand-red shadow-sm active:translate-y-[1px] transition-transform">
                    Modifier le mot de passe
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ---- ONGLET : API DIGISMS ---- */}
          {activeTab === 'api' && (
            <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-300">
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center mb-8">
                <h2 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-brand-dark flex items-center">
                  <Plug className="w-8 h-8 mr-3" /> Intégration API (DigiSMS)
                </h2>
                <div className="w-48 h-1 bg-brand-red rounded-full mt-3"></div>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Nom d'Expéditeur (Sender ID)</label>
                  <input type="text" defaultValue="DIGIALERT" className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-400 rounded-full text-brand-dark focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 outline-none" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Clé d'API (API Key)</label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <input 
                      type={showApiKey ? "text" : "password"} 
                      defaultValue="digisms_live_123456789" 
                      className="w-full pl-4 pr-12 py-3 border border-slate-400 rounded-full text-brand-dark focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 outline-none tracking-wider" 
                    />
                    <button onClick={() => setShowApiKey(!showApiKey)} className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-500 active:text-brand-dark">
                      {showApiKey ? <EyeOff className="w-6 h-6" /> : <Eye className="w-6 h-6" />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-6">
                  <button type="button" onClick={testConnection} className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-3 px-6 py-3 bg-white text-brand-dark font-extrabold text-xl rounded-2xl border-b-4 border-brand-red shadow-sm transition-all active:translate-y-[1px]">
                    {loadingConnection ? <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin text-brand-dark" /> : null}
                    <span>Tester la connexion</span>
                    <span className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full ${connectionStatus === 'success' ? 'bg-green-500 border-2 border-green-700 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(34,197,94,0.6)]' : connectionStatus === 'error' ? 'bg-red-500 border-2 border-red-700 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(239,68,68,0.4)]' : 'bg-slate-300 border border-slate-400'}`}></span>
                  </button>
                  <p className={`text-sm font-bold text-center mt-3 ${connectionStatus === 'error' ? 'text-red-600' : connectionStatus === 'success' ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-slate-600'}`}>{connectionMessage}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex justify-center pt-8">
                  <button className="px-8 py-3 bg-white text-brand-dark font-extrabold text-xl rounded-2xl border-b-4 border-brand-red shadow-sm active:translate-y-[1px] transition-transform">
                    Sauvegarder la clés API
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ---- ONGLET : TEMPLATES ---- */}
          {activeTab === 'templates' && (
            <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-300">
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center mb-8">
                <h2 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-brand-dark flex items-center">
                  <FileText className="w-8 h-8 mr-3" /> Modèles de SMS (Templates)
                </h2>
                <div className="w-48 h-1 bg-brand-red rounded-full mt-3"></div>
              </div>

              {/* Bloc "Vos Messages" avec bordure coupée */}
              <div className="max-w-lg mx-auto relative border-2 border-brand-dark rounded-3xl p-6 mt-6">
                <div className="absolute -top-4 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 bg-brand-dark text-white px-6 py-1 rounded-full font-bold text-lg">
                  Vos Messages
                </div>
                
                <div className="space-y-4 mt-2 max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto pr-2">
                  {templates.map((tpl) => (
                    <div key={tpl.id} className="flex justify-between items-center border-2 border-brand-red rounded-full px-6 py-3 bg-white shadow-sm">
                      <span className="font-extrabold text-lg text-brand-dark">{tpl.name}</span>
                      <div className="flex space-x-3">
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => { setEditingTemplate(tpl); setModelName(tpl.name); setMessageContent(tpl.content); setIsModalOpen(true); setConfirmDeleteId(null); }} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-brand-dark active:scale-95 transition-transform">
                          <Edit2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => setConfirmDeleteId(tpl.id)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-brand-red active:scale-95 transition-transform">
                          <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
                {confirmDeleteId && (
                  <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4">
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold text-red-700">Voulez-vous vraiment supprimer ce modèle ?</p>
                    <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3">
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => { setTemplates(prev => prev.filter(tpl => tpl.id !== confirmDeleteId)); setConfirmDeleteId(null); }} className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-full font-bold active:translate-y-[1px] transition-all">Supprimer</button>
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => setConfirmDeleteId(null)} className="px-4 py-2 bg-white text-brand-dark border border-slate-300 rounded-full font-bold active:translate-y-[1px] transition-all">Annuler</button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-center pt-10">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => {
                    setEditingTemplate(null);
                    setModelName('');
                    setMessageContent('');
                    setIsModalOpen(true);
                  }}
                  className="px-8 py-3 bg-white text-brand-dark font-extrabold text-xl rounded-2xl border-b-4 border-brand-red shadow-sm transition-all active:translate-y-[1px]"
                >
                  Nouveau Modèle
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ================= MODAL : NOUVEAU MODÈLE ================= */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-dark/40 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-[2.5rem] shadow-2xl w-full max-w-2xl p-10 relative animate-in zoom-in duration-200">

            {/* Header Modal */}
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center mb-8 relative">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} className="absolute right-0 top-0 text-slate-500 active:text-brand-dark">
                <X className="w-8 h-8" />
              </button>
              <h2 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-dark mb-2">
                {editingTemplate ? 'Modifier le Modèle' : 'Nouveau Modèle'}
              </h2>
              <div className="w-40 h-1.5 bg-brand-red rounded-full"></div>
            </div>

            {/* Nom du Modèle */}
            <div className="mb-8">
              <label className="block text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Nom du Modèle</label>
              <input type="text" value={modelName} onChange={e => setModelName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Rapelle Mr Emanuel" className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-400 rounded-full text-slate-600 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-red/20 outline-none" />
            </div>

            {/* Flex pour Texte et Champs */}
            <div className="flex gap-6 mb-8">
              <div className={`flex-1 relative border-[3px] border-brand-dark rounded-2xl p-6 mt-4 ${dragOverField ? 'bg-slate-50 border-dashed border-brand-red' : ''}`} onDragOver={e => { e.preventDefault(); setDragOverField(true); }} onDragLeave={() => setDragOverField(false)} onDrop={e => { e.preventDefault(); const field = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'); if (field) setMessageContent(prev => `${prev}${prev ? ' ' : ''}{${field}}`); setDragOverField(false); }}>
                <div className="absolute -top-4 left-6 bg-white px-2 font-extrabold text-lg text-brand-dark">
                  Contenu du message
                </div>
                <div className="text-slate-500 text-lg">
                  ex: Salut <span className="bg-rose-200 border border-brand-red text-slate-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full mx-1">Prenom</span> <span className="bg-rose-200 border border-brand-red text-slate-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full mx-1">Nom</span><br />
                  Votre rendez-vous est pour le 11/09/20
                </div>
                <textarea value={messageContent} onChange={e => setMessageContent(e.target.value)} className="w-full h-32 mt-4 resize-none outline-none text-xl font-medium text-slate-600" placeholder="Message"></textarea>
              </div>

              <div className="w-1/3 relative border-[3px] border-brand-dark rounded-2xl p-6 mt-4">
                <div className="absolute -top-4 left-6 bg-white px-2 font-extrabold text-lg text-brand-dark">
                  Champs
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col space-y-3 mt-2">
                  {fields.map((field) => (
                    <div key={field} className="bg-rose-200 border border-brand-red text-slate-700 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full font-medium w-max cursor-move" draggable onDragStart={e => e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', field)}>
                      {field}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-center gap-4 pt-2">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsModalOpen(false); setEditingTemplate(null); setConfirmDeleteId(null); }} className="px-10 py-3 bg-white text-brand-dark font-extrabold text-xl rounded-2xl border-b-4 border-brand-red shadow-sm transition-all active:translate-y-[1px]">
                {editingTemplate ? 'Annuler' : 'Fermer'}
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => {
                if (editingTemplate) {
                  setTemplates(prev => prev.map(tpl => tpl.id === editingTemplate.id ? { ...tpl, name: modelName || tpl.name, content: messageContent } : tpl));
                } else {
                  const nextId = templates.length ? Math.max(...templates.map(t => t.id)) + 1 : 1;
                  setTemplates(prev => [...prev, { id: nextId, name: modelName || `Modèle ${nextId}`, content: messageContent }]);
                }
                setIsModalOpen(false);
                setEditingTemplate(null);
              }} className="px-10 py-3 bg-white text-brand-dark font-extrabold text-xl rounded-2xl border-b-4 border-brand-red shadow-sm transition-all active:translate-y-[1px]">
                {editingTemplate ? 'Modifier' : 'Créer le Modèle'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default Parametres;

-------------------------------------------services--------------------------------------------
api.js : 

// src/services/api.js

// L'adresse de base de ton API (tu pourras la changer via un fichier .env plus tard pour la prod)
export const BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:5294';

export const fetchWithAuth = async (endpoint, options = {}) => {
    // 1. On récupère le badge (Token) s'il existe dans le stockage du navigateur
    const token = localStorage.getItem('jwtToken');
    
    // 2. On prépare les en-têtes (Headers)
    const headers = {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...options.headers,
    };

    // 3. Si on a un Token, on l'attache au format officiel : "Bearer <token>"
    if (token) {
        headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }

    // 4. On lance la requête
    const response = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
        ...options,
        headers,
    });

    // 5. Sécurité : Si l'API nous jette (Code 401 Unauthorized), c'est que le token est mort
    if (response.status === 401) {
        localStorage.removeItem('jwtToken');
        localStorage.removeItem('userId');
        window.location.href = '/login'; // On force le retour à la page de connexion
    }

    return response;
};
